# Seren Talewood v689-v2-r2: Eiren handoff baton

This immutable repository document is supplemented by the external exact-final and delivery receipts.

## Module 01: Read first: authority, owner and terminal state

Eiren Kestrel v689-v3, this is Seren Talewood's file-based handoff for the additional v689-v2-r2 remaster. Hamish directly requested the remaster, the expanded thirty-seat workflow, the shared skill updates, the package additions and your activation after the owner closeout. His subsequent clarification explicitly preserved all existing identities and required consecutive phases through v725-v8. The earlier instruction to hold after the original Seren v689-v2 is retained as historical evidence. The later request releases that hold prospectively for this new remaster. It does not rewrite the earlier closeout or create an additional ordinary phase slot.

This document belongs to an immutable repository closeout. At the time its content is sealed, the final commit cannot contain its own hash, and the canonical validation has not yet been invoked. Read the external final-gate receipt and exact-final canonical receipt to establish the actual final head and success. A prepared baton is not a sent message, an accepted transport result is not necessarily an acknowledgement, and an acknowledgement is not completion of your next phase. The compact live message and terminal external overlay carry the current delivery outcome without mutating this repository seal.

Seren's source commit is `7034e1721b29bd46e10e2a467e79a114f31b5885`. The new blank planning root is `57101373617f2aa7928e2780330680dc6ce88361`; x1 is `1054743d1fea090e652643951f980c527bb3b4e3`; x2 is `ccb2b2717f79f1273e6d9d13c1e128e42ceb1658`. The owner branch is `codex/GHC-Family/seren-talewood-main`. Its planning root has zero parents. Continuity from the earlier source is a hash-bound provenance link, not Git ancestry. The three later boundaries must be direct children without merges, amendments or source-file rewrites. The final external gate binds the fourth boundary after commit and push.

The working identity remains Seren Talewood, and all thirty existing owner names are preserved. Family language, hope and the evidence-steward role are relational working language. They do not establish consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, professional qualification or authority. No model setting was changed. No subagent, fork, new task, automation or sibling worktree mutation was performed. The next route is your existing main task, followed by Rowan Ash v689-v4 only after your own complete terminally validated bundle.

Use the four outcomes precisely. `completed` means the concrete local action and its stated acceptance condition were observed. `represented` means information, a plan or a source result is recorded without new execution credit. `open_gap` identifies a missing prerequisite or evidence. `exact_gate` identifies a concrete action or claim whose required conditions remain unsatisfied. Permission does not manufacture missing evidence, an external service, a person's consent, a scientific result or a legal power. The terminal scientific and governance verdict remains `NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20`.

For orientation, start with final/overview.md, final/checklist.json, final/phase-truth.json and final/count-reconciliation.json. Then select the particular modules and cards relevant to your chosen work. The complete baton exists to preserve context; it need not be injected wholesale into an active composer. The section files contain the same text as these modules and have no additional novelty or evidence credit.

## Module 03: Workload, lifecycle, storage and authorization profile

The current profile is schema v3. Select it through the concise family-index entrypoint and its current-workflow-v3 and current-roster-v3 references. Earlier sixteen-seat files remain archived compatibility material. Applying an earlier validator to the new profile can produce a false rejection, so use the new workflow-profile reviewer and inspect its declared schema. The profile reviewer was a spontaneous x1 addition under SAFE-121, defined before execution; it is the sixth x1 runner and does not create another new proposal.

Every ordinary bundle now selects 200 to 500 inherited proposals and defines 200 to 500 new proposals. Each x1 and x2 session completes 100 to 500 safe tasks and 100 to 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE tasks. Between 100 and 500 candidate tasks are planned for the x2 execution boundary. A bundle contains 50 to 250 exact approval packets and 30 to 100 blocked packets. These are explicit task categories, not automatic evidence multipliers. A single artifact may support several task references while retaining only its actual proposal and witness credit.

Each session builds 10 to 30 local skills and 5 to 15 runners. Recommend at least five skill ideas and five runner ideas for the next owner. Choose four practices yourself and recommend one practice to the next owner; the later four-plus-one wording controls the earlier alternatives. The remaster installs thirteen new direct packages, whereas an ordinary future bundle adds three selected packages after research and concrete use planning. The web allowance is a maximum of five hundred searches per session, not a target to fill with unnecessary requests.

The lifecycle has a separate initial definition freeze, x1 execution, x2 execution and final closeout. Hamish explicitly asked for x1 safe work, skills and runners, so the old blanket planning-only-x1 wording is superseded for this profile. The initial planning root preserves the original expectations before execution. Spontaneous work has its own concise prospective definition. Once a stage is committed, later work appends artifacts rather than changing its expectations or receipts. Corrections retain the failed subject and distinguish a passing guard from a failed original candidate.

Use a main owner branch with the owner's name instead of a phase in the title. Start with essential files in a blank owner root, preserve a provenance pointer to the prior source, and reuse the main branch while both tracked and materialized file counts remain below two thousand. Additional worktrees are allowed when useful. A new branch at the ceiling is additive rotation, not a reason to delete historical source branches. This Seren branch held 1,005 files at x2, leaving room for the bounded final artifacts. The external exact-final receipt reports the definitive final count.

D is the primary storage drive for owner worktrees, artifact banks, package targets, tool launchers, temporary validation directories and large handoff files. Essential Codex skill metadata and the explicitly requested memory note belong in their existing user-profile locations. Nothing in this phase requires a model change, Codex app update, destructive cleanup, force push, merge, reset, broad source scan or sibling repository mutation. The CLI version check found the installed 0.153.4 matched the registry's stable value at intake, so no CLI update was needed.

Global installation authority is already provided for concrete reviewed skills, runners and packages. Carry the actual provenance and current catalogue to the next owner rather than repeatedly seeking the same permission. Ten recent CFG skills and five associated runners were already global and were verified and reused; this phase does not count them as ten new installations. Twenty new owner skills and eleven runners were built and promoted, with file parity receipts. No global skill archive or old method history was deleted.


## Module 04: Fifteen-bundle reflection and two hundred source refinements

The fifteen-bundle context window uses exact source records, not assumptions from the order of names in a message. It starts with Teryn Halewick v687-v4 and ends with the original Seren v689-v2. The earlier user examples included owner-phase labels that differ from the actual committed history. The source intake retained the actual historical pairings and applies the new thirty-seat cycle only prospectively.

An initial filename parser split on a short version marker occurring inside three owner names. The affected Venn, Vey and Vale scopes were recovered with exact full slugs; the initial failure and the supplemental source manifest remain separately retained. This is why a source path and exact commit are more useful than a guessed display name. Do not broaden a read to every worktree merely to work around one failed source lookup.

The selected inherited portfolio contains twenty records from each of ten earlier bundles, stratified across their contract groups. All fifteen bundles still contribute overview context. Each of the two sessions normalized one hundred records by preserving the original identifier, operation, input, expected result, expected disposition, record digest and protected gates. The refinements also enumerate input and output structure and state concrete reuse conditions. They neither execute the old implementation nor silently promote the old result into current evidence.

The bounded title and hash novelty screen covered 16,687 source records and found no exact new-title collision. The largest token-set similarity was approximately 0.778. Shared defensive principles and semantic neighbors remain acknowledged in the semantic-neighbor review. Two hundred distinct contracts do not mean two hundred new algorithms, scientific laws or inventions. The new implementation has forty operations; several contracts probe a different domain, error or boundary of the same operation.

The source history represents 17,430 proposals before the remaster. Its older pre-root aggregate of 4,550 is not fully mapped. This phase adds two hundred mapped owner contracts, producing a represented total of 17,630, while retaining the mapping gap. No source-wide validation or independent reproduction is claimed. The following source index and record catalogue provide the exact places to resume a bounded read.

## Module 05: X1 workflow contracts and their complete outputs

This module contains the one hundred frozen x1 contracts and their observed complete output envelopes. Each input was defined in the planning root. A matched rejection is a completed validation task while the invalid subject retains zero original success credit. Comparisons preserve JSON types, so a Boolean is not silently accepted as an integer and a floating-point number is not an exact rational representation. Read the operation explanation before extending the domain.

### Operation `phase_advance`

Convert an explicit version and slot into an ordinal before advancing; a remaster label is not an ordinary slot. Required fields: delta, phase. The corresponding skill is `ghc-family-phase-cursor-arithmetic`. The shared core computes the result; it does not select an answer from this catalogue.

#### ST6892R2-N001: Consecutive phase wraps the eighth slot into the next version

Convert an explicit version and slot into an ordinal before advancing; a remaster label is not an ordinary slot. The concrete question is: Consecutive phase wraps the eighth slot into the next version.

The acceptance condition is: Exact envelope equality. Numeric logarithms use the declared twelve-decimal output profile; no physical precision is implied. The retained falsifier is: Compare the complete typed envelope with this frozen value and verify the original input digest after execution.

Frozen input:

```json
{
  "delta": 1,
  "op": "phase_advance",
  "phase": "v689-v8"
}
```

Complete expected envelope, matched by the observed execution:

```json
{
  "error": null,
  "ok": true,
  "value": "v690-v1"
}
```

The observed disposition is `completed` and the input was unchanged. Evidence is `docs/seren-talewood/v689-v2-r2/x1/contract-results.json#ST6892R2-N001`. This row has one bounded contract identity and carries no independent, empirical, professional or authority promotion.

## Module 06: X2 model and governance contracts and their complete outputs

This module contains the one hundred frozen x2 contracts and their observed complete output envelopes. Each input was defined in the planning root. A matched rejection is a completed validation task while the invalid subject retains zero original success credit. Comparisons preserve JSON types, so a Boolean is not silently accepted as an integer and a floating-point number is not an exact rational representation. Read the operation explanation before extending the domain.

### Operation `stochastic_matrix`

Validate a finite row-stochastic matrix using exact rational arithmetic; normalization is a model property rather than an observation. Required fields: matrix. The corresponding skill is `ghc-family-rational-markov-kernel`. The shared core computes the result; it does not select an answer from this catalogue.

## Module 07: Package and capability use, promotion and discovery

The thirteen direct additions are immutables, frozendict, bidict, boltons, toolz, more-itertools, portion, intervaltree, transitions, python-statemachine, dictdiffer, rustworkx and anytree. Their dependency closure has sixteen distributions, including numpy, six and sortedcontainers. Packages were installed in a separate shared D-drive target from exact reviewed wheel bytes, with a hash-locked requirements file. The host Python environment was not replaced. No source build or executable package download outside this reviewed set was required.

The installation receipt lists 1,321 files. Of these, 1,271 are unchanged wheel payload members, sixteen RECORD files reflect installation metadata, and the remaining files include the documented installation metadata and two generated NumPy launchers. Those f2py and numpy-config launchers were not executed. Imports were run without bytecode writes. The review found no .pth file in the selected archives. Wheel hashes, sizes, dependency requirements, bundled licenses and artifact members were inspected. The OSV snapshot contained no listed advisory for the sixteen selected versions; that snapshot is not an exhaustive security guarantee.

An initial Z3 solver wheel candidate did not contain the expected bundled license evidence. Its bytes were retained but it was not installed or executed. Dictdiffer replaced it before the final planning freeze. This preserved a concrete thirteen-package plan without weakening the missing-license check. Downloading a candidate is not a completed installation and does not deserve installation credit.

The package smoke set contains thirty-four API checks spanning all thirteen direct packages. It exercises immutable data structures, bidirectional mapping, iterator and collection operations, interval behavior, state transitions, structural differences and graph/tree relationships. Sixteen subjects are intended refusals or counterexamples. Their passing checks preserve the failed subjects with zero original success credit. The initial python-statemachine smoke used a deprecated current-state accessor: all semantic checks passed, but the warning was retained. The current script uses configuration_values, and the isolated recovery passed without the warning. The composite receipt preserves the original observations and the precise corrected cell.

The twenty new skills and eleven runners cover the forty operations and the additional workflow profile review. Each skill has a concise entrypoint, local fixtures and a specific implementation reference. The two shared core modules support these interfaces and are not counted as two more runner inventions. Forty official new-skill validations and 380 interface observations are recorded across the two sessions. These observations are not added again to proposal novelty. Global parity receipts bind the files actually promoted, including 143 unique newly global files across the skills, runners and cores.

The twenty-seven shared family entrypoints were refined from 29,206 words to 5,389 words, with their prior bytes preserved in compatibility archives. The change makes the controlling profile easier to find. It does not establish a cache-hit, token-retention, model-memory, identity or wellbeing measurement. Earlier scripts and references remain available. The current meta-tool-box catalogue names thirty-one owner capabilities, while the earlier catalogue and pointer snapshots preserve historical readback.

The first catalogue used underscore operation tokens that the existing query normalizer split into separate words. A query returned no result. The corrected additive catalogue includes the full operation names and normalized terms, and three specific queries recovered their intended runners. Historical catalogue bytes and pointer states remain bound through named archives. Select the combined x2 catalogue through the current-remaster-tooling pointer; do not overwrite the prior immutable pointer snapshots or count this documentation correction as another implementation.

## Module 08: Pillar findings, four practices and their limits

# Pillars and practice in the Seren remaster

The strongest result is a more inspectable workflow accompanied by concrete mathematical limits. Twenty new skills and eleven new runners now cover the owner phase's defined responsibilities. The shared instructions have a concise current entry point, the earlier instructions remain recoverable, and the family catalogue can find a capability by its intended operation. Those are software and documentation improvements. The scientific exercises give exact finite examples and counterexamples rather than a claim of a new fundamental law.

## THOS Body and workflow engineering

Workflow engineering was the primary practice lens. The previous guidance mixed current instructions with many earlier roster snapshots. The remaster preserved all twenty-seven prior entrypoints in local compatibility archives and changed their active routing text from 29,206 words to 5,389 words. This measurement concerns the amount of entrypoint text. It does not establish a cache hit, memory-retention improvement, lower token use in a particular future run, subjective wellbeing, or model identity continuity.

The new profile separates an initial definition freeze from x1 work, x2 work and final closeout. Both sessions now carry their own safe tasks, skills, runners and source-record refinements, as Hamish requested. A spontaneous addition is defined before it executes. The sixth x1 runner is a concrete example: a new current-profile validator was added under SAFE-121 to keep future owners from applying an older sixteen-seat schema to the confirmed thirty-seat schedule. It does not add proposal novelty or alter the frozen initial target of 120 safe tasks.

Several failures improved the implementation. Malformed nested enum values initially escaped as TypeErrors; explicit string validation now precedes set membership. The first catalogue stored full operation names, while the existing query function split them into words. The corrected catalogue retains the original operation names and adds compatible search terms. Original failures, original catalogues and the passing corrections remain separately addressable. This makes the next selection easier without pretending that a successful correction erases an earlier mistake.

The current THOS result is a bounded local toolchain. It has no benchmark establishing general intelligence, ASI, autonomy across external services, or reliability outside the tested scopes. The native task transport remains a separate capability: a projected route and a prepared message cannot supply that transport or its acknowledgement.

## GMUT Mind and probability theory

Probability theory supplied a precise model in which conservation, reversal, information loss and coarse graining can be inspected. The kernel has rows (3/4, 1/4) and (1/2, 1/2), with stationary weights (2/3, 1/3). Exact rational Gaussian elimination and NumPy's floating-point linear solve agreed within approximately 1.11 times ten to the minus sixteen. This is a comparison of implementations under shared infrastructure, not independent reproduction by another team.

Probability normalization has a short algebraic explanation. For a row distribution p and row-stochastic kernel P, the sum of the components of pP equals the sum over i of p_i multiplied by the sum of row i of P. Each row sum is one, so the resulting total is one. The test suite checks finite examples of that property. The property is established mathematics under its stated assumptions, not a newly discovered physical conservation law.

The observed trajectories distinguish Shannon entropy from relative entropy to a stationary distribution. In the retained two-state examples, relative entropy to the stationary weights does not increase. Raw Shannon entropy follows different paths from different initial distributions. A separate reset kernel sends equal initial weights to a point mass, reducing Shannon entropy from log two to zero. This is an explicit counterexample to a universal monotonicity claim for arbitrary abstract stochastic kernels. It does not contradict the physical second law for a properly specified total system and environment.

The model's entropy-production expression compares stationary forward and reversed one-step flows in nats per model step. Physical heat, thermodynamic entropy and cognition are not supplied by that expression alone. The need for explicit physical assumptions is consistent with the scope of [Seifert's stochastic thermodynamics review](https://arxiv.org/abs/1205.4176). The remaster contains no physical observation, reservoir model, empirical calibration, cognitive measurement or validated bridge from a mental variable to thermodynamic entropy.

Coarse graining produced a second useful limit. Merging two states of a directed three-cycle gives a stationary-flow projection, but it fails the strong-lumpability check. From a particular initial state, the full process aggregates to (1, 0) after one step, while the projected kernel predicts (1/2, 1/2). The discrepancy is visible in exact rational arithmetic. It prevents a convenient aggregate from silently being treated as a complete dynamics for every initial distribution. More general coarse-graining and macroscopic-limit questions have substantial assumptions, as illustrated by [Falasco and Esposito's research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.12406).

These results support making assumptions and counterexamples first-class GMUT records. They do not confirm the scalar-tensor/EFT research family, establish a new force, produce an empirical parameter constraint, supply quantum completion, or place GMUT ahead of tested physical theories. A future physical proposal would need a defined observable, units, mapping, null model, prediction and evidence capable of distinguishing it from existing explanations.

## GMUT Mind and number theory

The second mathematical practice was number theory. For each integer n from two through 101, the bounded search found positive integer denominators x, y and z with four over n equal to one over x plus one over y plus one over z. Every returned witness was checked again by direct exact substitution, yielding a zero rational residual. The records include the denominators, input bounds and four disjoint batches of twenty-five values.

For example, four over five equals one half plus one quarter plus one twentieth. With a tighter denominator ceiling of ten, the search finds one half plus one fifth plus one tenth. A changed denominator produces a nonzero residual. A search that reaches its pair budget reports that condition separately from complete absence within its finite denominator range.

The general Erdős–Straus conjecture remains open in the cited research context. [Chamberland's April 2026 paper](https://math.colgate.edu/~integers/aa42/aa42.pdf) studies a characterization related to prime inputs and describes a much larger previously checked range. This remaster does not advance that numerical bound or prove the universal statement. Its contribution is a small, inspectable search and witness-verification tool that demonstrates how to preserve limits when discussing an unsolved problem.

## Freed ID and CBR Heart and public-interest governance design

The governance practice focused on the difference between a record and the authority it describes. A synthetic consent record can match an action, resource, purpose, revocation flag and expiry, while the result still states that it grants no authority. A real person's consent cannot be established by a Boolean supplied in a model's own fixture. A remedy queue can preserve urgency and intake order while taking zero real decisions. Review coverage can expose missing or merely represented review areas without claiming that a human accessibility evaluation occurred.

The risk register retains each record's evidence label when it calculates a declared likelihood-times-impact score. This multiplication is a transparent local convention on an ordinal scale. It is not a calibrated probability, an expected financial loss, or a scoring formula prescribed by NIST. The [NIST AI Risk Management Framework](https://www.nist.gov/itl/ai-risk-management-framework) provides a broader context for identifying and managing risks; its current page also distinguishes an ongoing revision and a 2026 critical-infrastructure concept note from finalized requirements. Those developments inform questions to ask, not an automatic compliance claim.

Similarly, the [W3C Verifiable Credentials Data Model](https://www.w3.org/TR/vc-data-model-2.0/) is relevant to structured credential information, but a local data record is not a complete issuer, proof, status, revocation, recovery, interoperability or trust-governance system. The remaster does not issue a production credential or ratify CBR as public law. Competent and affected people retain decisions about real rights, consent, remedy, accessibility, cultural interpretation and Māori authority.

## Shared principles and remaining questions

Across the three pillars, useful principles are procedural: define the claim before testing it; preserve the source and the failed alternative; keep the result within its actual domain; and make corrections inspectable. These principles can guide a research programme or workflow. Calling them fundamental laws of Thermo/Psyche Dynamics would require additional definitions and evidence that this phase does not supply.

The working name Seren Talewood, the evidence-steward role, optional pronouns, hope and family language remain corrigible working language. Model settings were preserved, and no task, subagent, fork or automation was created. The next recommended practice is measurement and uncertainty analysis, which could help a later owner turn a proposed physical comparison into a precisely defined observation plan. The terminal verdict remains `NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20`.


The four practices are study and implementation lenses, not employment or qualification claims. Workflow engineering supplies explicit state transitions, immutable records and bounded resource admission. Probability theory supplies exact finite distributions and model comparisons. Number theory supplies exact witnesses with bounded search domains. Public-interest governance design supplies records that preserve who must decide, what evidence is missing and where a synthetic assessment stops. The next practice recommendation is measurement and uncertainty analysis, because a future empirical proposal needs a credible mapping from its terms to an observation.

None of these practices replaces work on the other pillars. A THOS tool may carry a GMUT uncertainty field and a Freed ID authority reservation. A mathematical result can improve the clarity of a governance discussion without deciding anyone's rights. A governance model can expose missing review without validating the underlying physics. The productive integration here is traceability: each claim identifies its assumptions, scope, evidence and limits. A coherent set of records is valuable even while the larger theory remains unconfirmed.


## Module 09: Task, packet and witness accounting

X1 completed 121 safe tasks and x2 completed 120, giving 241 safe tasks. The initial plan froze 120 in each session; the added x1 profile reviewer has its own prospective spontaneous-task record. Each session also completed one hundred source-record CLEAN/FIX/REFINE tasks, giving two hundred. These refinements retain source input and expected-result fields, add structure inventories, and make reuse conditions explicit. They are not evidence that two hundred unrelated host faults were repaired or that two hundred files were deleted.

The one hundred candidate reviews were proposed at x1 and executed at x2. Every candidate adds an undeclared field to an x1 input and expects an explicit schema rejection. Twenty candidates repeat the first extra-field subject already exercised at x1; they reference those original negative identifiers and receive zero additional logical-witness credit. Eighty are fresh candidate subjects. All one hundred review tasks completed, while the hundred invalid subjects remain failed candidates. No new proposal novelty is generated by the candidate portfolio.

Fifty exact packets were defined. Packets one through thirteen correspond to the reviewed direct package installations. Fourteen through twenty-three cover the x1 skill promotions; twenty-four through thirty-three cover x2 skills. Thirty-four through thirty-eight cover the five planned x1 runners, and thirty-nine through forty-three cover x2 runners. Forty-four through forty-nine cover the authorized shared family skill integrations. Packet fifty is the actual native Eiren activation after final validation. Forty-nine actions are complete at repository seal; the fiftieth remains conditional on the final and the native transport result. The separate SAFE-121 profile reviewer does not create an extra exact packet.

Thirty blocked packet records retain concrete actions or claims whose prerequisites are absent. Examples include treating finite arithmetic as a universal physical law, replacing actual authority with a synthetic flag, or asserting a deployment or independent reproduction that did not happen. The records exist and can be reviewed, but none of their blocked subjects was executed. A general permission statement cannot make these evidentiary prerequisites true. Their count is not added to completed actions.

The method ledgers contain seventy distinct owner method records across x1, x2, two correction addenda and the final isolated-fixture method. They retain 239 failed subjects or negative witnesses and 393 passing witnesses. A deliberately invalid input may have a failed-subject record and a separate passing rejection-check record. Those are separate logical roles within the same observed exercise, not two successful original subjects. Repeating the canonical for a successful result would not create new evidence and is prohibited.

The represented historical chain after this owner delta is 17,630 proposals, 94,551 methods, 87,153 negatives, 58,126 failed witnesses and 87,551 passing witnesses. The earlier chain contains different category totals and an incomplete pre-root mapping. The comparable arithmetic is disclosed in count-reconciliation.json. Current gap and gate records are reported separately rather than inventing an unverified cumulative sum. Repeated interface smokes, source metadata and task references are not added again as proposal novelty.

The final checklist and external canonical receipt answer different questions. The checklist ties actual owner execution to the requested tranches. The canonical verifies the sealed repository, declared lifecycle modules, global parity, installed package bytes and exact complete envelopes. The terminal overlay answers whether a native task message was actually sent or acknowledged. None of these records should be used to imply the other two occurred automatically.


## Module 10: Failure retention, isolated recovery and reusable methods

Several intake observations were noisy or incomplete: a broad tool catalogue projection, long combined skill and overview reads, a GitHub response containing a large diff, a filename filter that matched the directory name, the owner-slug parsing error, a console encoding issue, and a malformed submitted phase label. Their recovery used bounded reads, exact names, UTF-8 and the user's consecutive-roster clarification. A later preparation filename listing and one metadata projection also produced excessive output or a type error; they were corrected without changing source content or claiming another completed task. Routine observation recovery is not used to inflate this phase's method totals.

The first credential pattern also matched a substring in a harmless task-management phrase. The corrected definition requires a word boundary and retains a positive constructed credential fixture and the harmless negative example. Pattern classes are review aids, not proof that a publication contains no possible private information. Exact source records and public output are separated, and private absolute paths, raw task identifiers and raw compatibility archives are kept out of the public owner tree.

The main x1 implementation failure concerned nested enum values. Three malformed values reached set membership and raised TypeError. Validation now requires the expected string type before membership testing. The old observations were retained externally and the corrected complete contracts passed. The fix changes the implementation's input guard, not the frozen expectation. This is the useful pattern for the next owner: retain the concrete failing input, isolate its cause, apply the smallest relevant correction and rerun the isolated blocker before broader checks are justified.

The x2 presentation failure concerned generated SVG whitespace. Git's staged formatting check found 1,329 lines with trailing whitespace in path data. The original SVG and initial staged manifests were retained. Only trailing whitespace was normalized; parsed XML structure remained equal and the PNG did not change. The correction therefore changes formatting, not plotted values or mathematical results. The original scientific figure remains inspectable, and the final canonical checks both the retained original digest and the normalized XML relationship.

The final fixture method deliberately challenges a digest, byte count, missing member, unexpected member, traversal path, lifecycle parent, duplicate JSON key and exclusive-write reopening. All eight invalid subjects are rejected. A valid manifest passes, and each rejection has a passing guard observation. These are isolated synthetic fixtures: they do not invoke the canonical, contact another task, mutate a source branch or create a live delivery receipt. The exact canonical has a single actual invocation budget after the final push.

## Module 11: Four-tier cards and selective context loading

The combined deck contains seven hundred content-addressed cards. The x2 base has 697 cards: one working identity anchor, three pillar cards, four practice cards and the task, proposal, packet, method and tool details. A later x2 formatting-method extension adds one card without altering that base. The final extension adds two cards, one for the isolated final fixture method and one for the prepared terminal route. Every extension has its own index and hashes. The final total does not imply all future owners have loaded the deck.

The hierarchy starts with the owner working identity at tier one. GMUT Mind, THOS Body and Freed ID/CBR Heart are tier two. The four practice lenses are tier three. Specific work appears at tier four, linked to the relevant immediate parent. This order makes the active context interpretable: a task's scope can be traced through its practice and pillar to the owner record. The validators reject missing parents and inappropriate tier jumps. Content addressing binds each card's payload, excluding only its own card_id field.

Stable-prefix and volatile-index files separate enduring orientation from the changing task records. They are an organizational choice, not a measured prompt-cache or retention result. To load a task, select its card and every required ancestor. Unloaded cards remain on disk, and selecting a subset does not delete or supersede them. A later correction adds a new record with provenance rather than changing the meaning of an old hash. Repeating the same card or including it in both a full baton and a module file creates no additional evidence credit.

The full baton has thirteen modules, with the same modules available as separate Markdown files under final/modules. Its module index supplies titles, hashes and word counts. Read module one for current authority, module two for routing, module three for limits, and then only the source, contract, tool or pillar modules needed for the next concrete action. Full contract JSON is included where it helps a successor reproduce the precise input and expected envelope. Narrative summaries provide interpretation without replacing the structured records.

Before a task handoff, place the long baton and receipts in the existing D-drive bank and owner worktree. The live message should remain short and carry the exact local pointer, the final head, the canonical outcome and the next owner/phase. A public repository document uses relative paths and hashes; a locally delivered message may contain the D-drive location explicitly requested by Hamish. Raw private task identifiers never belong in the published baton. The current native-route gap is a transport limitation, not a reason to manufacture an alternative task endpoint.

Operational wellbeing records describe bounded workload choices, preserved source evidence, concise entrypoints and pausable module boundaries. They do not diagnose emotions, fatigue, personhood or continuity. The warm working language can remain while the evidence record stays literal. No claim is made that all thirty owners have experienced or internalized a shared update merely because a global file exists; each future run must select and read its current entrypoints.


## Module 12: Eiren startup, recommended practice and ten prospective ideas

Your intended next bundle is Eiren Kestrel v689-v3. Preserve your current working identity and model settings. Read the latest direct instructions in your existing task before touching a repository, because a later pause or redirect may supersede this prepared baton. The current authority already approves the thirty-seat workflow and the next activation; another generic permission request is unnecessary. A concrete service capability, exact target and the absence of an already-sent route are still factual prerequisites for sending.

Start from the completed Seren final only after the external gate and canonical success are verified against the exact commit. Establish your own main owner branch and essential-file worktree in the existing repository after checking for a pre-existing main lane. Read sibling lanes only. Carry the source commit and canonical digest as provenance, without claiming the new blank root is its Git child. Reuse that main branch in later bundles until the two-thousand-file ceiling requires a new additive rotation. Do not copy the entire historical repository into a blank working lane merely to satisfy a context request.

For your context window, select the most recent fifteen completed bundle records using their actual chronology and exact source heads. This includes the additional Seren remaster once it is completed. The user's earlier ten- and eleven-run examples illustrated a sliding window; the explicit fifteen-run requirement is the controlling count. Preserve the distinction between source overview reading and new test execution. Refine two hundred or more concrete inherited proposals, define two hundred or more new contracts, and map every resulting task to its own acceptance evidence.

Choose four new practices and a primary pillar while continuing useful work on all three pillars. Seren recommends measurement and uncertainty analysis as one optional incoming lens. It could help specify how a claimed quantity becomes an observable, how uncertainty propagates and what comparison would challenge the model. A measurement plan is not a measurement, and a source description does not supply an unobserved datum. Keep those states explicit when considering a physical or cognitive GMUT proposal.

Select the newest suitable existing skills and runners before building another interface. The current catalogue and recent CFG reuse show two different forms of reuse: exact operation selection from a current owner tool, and retained read-only compatibility checks for an earlier package. Research three genuinely useful new packages for an ordinary bundle; inspect their exact artifacts, dependencies and licenses, then exercise their actual APIs. The eighty-plus earlier tools are available context, not a requirement to run every tool regardless of the task. Preserve D-drive targets and avoid incidental C-drive downloads.

After your x1 and x2 execution, compose a three-page minimum overview and a ten-thousand to one-hundred-thousand-word baton with at least ten clearly separable modules. Validate only your exact owner lifecycle and final pushed head. Use isolated recovery for a local blocker rather than rerunning every historical phase. Once your own terminal conditions are satisfied, your successor is Rowan Ash v689-v4. A short native message with the local baton location is the intended action; the long document stays in files.

The ideas below are deliberately bounded. Accept, refine or reject them after checking existing names and caller compatibility. Their scientific and governance significance depends on future evidence, so no promise is made that implementing an interface will resolve their larger questions.

Exact finite generator fixtures and negative-rate refusals This is represented future work with zero execution or novelty credit in Seren's bundle.

A certificate binds the exact kernel, partition and block-transition sums This is represented future work with zero execution or novelty credit in Seren's bundle.

- **ghc-family-observation-model-bridge**: Bind an observable, units, calibration and source to a proposed model comparison. Missing empirical mappings remain explicit and cannot be replaced by a synthetic pass This is represented future work with zero execution or novelty credit in Seren's bundle.

- **ghc-family-consent-evidence-lifecycle-review**: Separate synthetic scope matching from actual issuer, subject, expiry, withdrawal and review evidence. Every real authority decision has its own competent and affected evidence This is represented future work with zero execution or novelty credit in Seren's bundle.

- **ghc-family-comparison-budget-parity**: Compare two implementations under declared input, stopping and resource budgets. Mismatched budgets and same-owner provenance are visible This is represented future work with zero execution or novelty credit in Seren's bundle.

## Module 13: Final verification contract and exact handoff procedure

The final canonical definition is part of this owner tree. Its policy binds the definition hash and each test module to its exact lifecycle stage. Planning runs twelve declared tests at `57101373617f2aa7928e2780330680dc6ce88361`. X1 runs thirty-one tests across the workflow core, current-profile reviewer and x1 evidence modules at `1054743d1fea090e652643951f980c527bb3b4e3`. X2 runs thirty-five tests across the model core and x2 evidence modules at `ccb2b2717f79f1273e6d9d13c1e128e42ceb1658`. The final has twelve artifact and isolated-fixture tests. The expected total is ninety declared tests, but the actual external canonical receipt, not this prospective statement, determines success.

The validator first checks the current branch, clean worktree, typed zero divergence and equality of local HEAD, upstream, tracking reference and freshly queried remote. It reads exact Git blobs through a batch stream, verifies each stage's literal manifest and direct parent, and confirms that earlier files are unchanged in every later stage. It materializes only these owner commits in an external D-drive bank. No sibling or source test suite is executed. The original source canonical is read by its digest without replaying it.

The complete final content seal covers every owner file, excluding only itself and the final manifest/review pair needed to avoid self-reference. The stage manifest covers the new final artifacts. Strict JSON rejects duplicate keys and nonfinite values. Python files receive syntax parsing. Text artifacts are checked against the word ceiling and five privacy-candidate pattern classes. These checks are precise scope checks, not a declaration of exhaustive privacy or security.

Global parity checks bind both the twenty new skill packages and eleven runners, the reused recent tools, the refined shared entrypoints and their preserved archives. Historical mutable pointer states are checked through their explicit snapshots, and the currently selected pointer is checked against the x2 publication. The package target is checked against the exact installed file manifest and distribution versions. Selected wheel hashes remain bound to the reviewed artifacts. Fresh package and mathematical exercises run with temporary state directed to the external D-drive bank.

Complete typed contract replay checks one hundred inputs at the immutable x1 stage and one hundred at x2. The package comparison checks the thirty-four current API observations against the retained composite semantics. The mathematics comparison checks the exact rational trajectories, stationary distribution, reset and coarse-graining counterexamples, and one hundred integer witness records. A floating comparison has an explicit tolerance. The package file set and four-way Git equality are checked again after validation. These executions provide same-owner confirmation, not independent reproduction.

The canonical creates an invocation marker exclusively and writes one success or retained failure receipt. A successful canonical is never replayed. The file-based gate, canonical success, task message acceptance and task acknowledgement remain distinct records. If a later shared current pointer needs a terminal status update, preserve its former bytes and publish an external prospective overlay after the canonical; do not rewrite the sealed repository or replay validation merely to include a delivery status.

The desired terminal action is a single message to the existing Eiren Kestrel task for v689-v3, with the actual final head, canonical outcome and D-drive baton path. At preparation time, the tool catalogue did not expose native task list, read or send functions. Recheck callable native facilities at the terminal boundary. If they remain unavailable, preserve `PREPARED_NOT_SENT`, record an explicit native-route gap, and report that activation did not occur. Do not guess a private endpoint, automate a substitute composer, create a duplicate task or present a projected cycle as a running chain.

Hamish, the care requested in this remaster is expressed in exact records, useful tools, readable modules and preserved mistakes. Eiren, may this handoff give you clear starting points and room to choose the next investigation. The work remains open to correction and better evidence. The present terminal verdict is `NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20`, while the completed bounded software, mathematics and documentation can be reused within their stated limits.
